# Source-discovery selection decisions

This is the normalized content in its original document order with a source-discovery semantic overlay. The labels are annotations; the content beneath them remains the normalized source structure.

- **Green — SELECTED:** relevant, current material eligible for extraction.
- **Orange — SELECTED WITH UNCERTAINTY:** possibly relevant, time-bounded, or temporally unknown material that remains eligible for extraction.
- **Gray — HISTORICAL:** retained for audit but excluded from current terms.
- **Blue — FUTURE:** retained for audit but excluded from current terms.
- **White — NOT SELECTED / UNASSESSED:** unchanged content without an accepted current extraction decision.

&lt;div class=&quot;container&quot;&gt;  
    &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__row&quot;&gt;  
        &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary&quot;&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__content&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__header&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;h3&gt;Overdraft&lt;/h3&gt;  
                    &lt;img class=&quot;first-section&quot; src=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/Portals/0/Images/icons/card-loan-icon.png&quot; alt=&quot;overdraft-icon&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
                &lt;div&gt;  
                    &lt;p&gt;  
                        The overdraft provided by payment cards allows you to use the credit funds provided by the Bank without first depositing money into the card account.  
                    &lt;/p&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__footer&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;a href=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/en/personal/loans/consumer-loans/overdraft&quot; target=&quot;blank&quot;&gt;  
                    learn more  
                    &lt;svg width=&quot;14&quot; height=&quot;12&quot; viewBox=&quot;0 0 14 12&quot; fill=&quot;none&quot; xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/svg&quot;&gt;  
                        &lt;path d=&quot;M8.52175 1.33301L12.9998 5.99967L8.52175 10.6663M12.1552 6.02083H1.6665&quot; stroke=&quot;#121212&quot; stroke-width=&quot;2&quot; stroke-linecap=&quot;round&quot; stroke-linejoin=&quot;round&quot;/&gt;  
                    &lt;/svg&gt;  
                &lt;/a&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary&quot;&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__content&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__header&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;h3&gt;Credit line&lt;/h3&gt;  
                    &lt;img class=&quot;first-section&quot; src=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/Portals/0/Images/icons/credit-line-icon.png&quot; alt=&quot;credit-line-icon&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
                &lt;div&gt;  
                    &lt;p&gt;  
                        A credit line is a convenient way to have available funds in case of unplanned expenses and unplanned purchases.   
                    &lt;/p&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__footer&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;a href=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/en/personal/loans/consumer-loans/credit-line&quot; target=&quot;blank&quot;&gt;  
                    Learn more  
                    &lt;svg width=&quot;14&quot; height=&quot;12&quot; viewBox=&quot;0 0 14 12&quot; fill=&quot;none&quot; xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/svg&quot;&gt;  
                        &lt;path d=&quot;M8.52175 1.33301L12.9998 5.99967L8.52175 10.6663M12.1552 6.02083H1.6665&quot; stroke=&quot;#121212&quot; stroke-width=&quot;2&quot; stroke-linecap=&quot;round&quot; stroke-linejoin=&quot;round&quot;/&gt;  
                    &lt;/svg&gt;  
                &lt;/a&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary&quot;&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__content&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__header&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;h3&gt;MyPay&lt;/h3&gt;  
                    &lt;img class=&quot;first-section&quot; src=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/Portals/0/Images/icons/credit-limit-icon.png&quot; alt=&quot;credit-limit-icon&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
                &lt;div&gt;  
                    &lt;p&gt;  
                        MyPay limit is the amount pre-approved by the Bank that you can use to make payments at the Bank’s partner shops.  
  
  
                    &lt;/p&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;

&lt;div class=&quot;container&quot;&gt;  
    &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__row&quot;&gt;  
        &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary&quot;&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__content&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__header&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;h3&gt;Overdraft&lt;/h3&gt;  
                    &lt;img class=&quot;first-section&quot; src=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/Portals/0/Images/icons/card-loan-icon.png&quot; alt=&quot;overdraft-icon&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
                &lt;div&gt;  
                    &lt;p&gt;  
                        The overdraft provided by payment cards allows you to use the credit funds provided by the Bank without first depositing money into the card account.  
                    &lt;/p&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__footer&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;a href=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/en/personal/loans/consumer-loans/overdraft&quot; target=&quot;blank&quot;&gt;  
                    learn more  
                    &lt;svg width=&quot;14&quot; height=&quot;12&quot; viewBox=&quot;0 0 14 12&quot; fill=&quot;none&quot; xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/svg&quot;&gt;  
                        &lt;path d=&quot;M8.52175 1.33301L12.9998 5.99967L8.52175 10.6663M12.1552 6.02083H1.6665&quot; stroke=&quot;#121212&quot; stroke-width=&quot;2&quot; stroke-linecap=&quot;round&quot; stroke-linejoin=&quot;round&quot;/&gt;  
                    &lt;/svg&gt;  
                &lt;/a&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary&quot;&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__content&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__header&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;h3&gt;Credit line&lt;/h3&gt;  
                    &lt;img class=&quot;first-section&quot; src=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/Portals/0/Images/icons/credit-line-icon.png&quot; alt=&quot;credit-line-icon&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
                &lt;div&gt;  
                    &lt;p&gt;  
                        A credit line is a convenient way to have available funds in case of unplanned expenses and unplanned purchases.   
                    &lt;/p&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__footer&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;a href=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/en/personal/loans/consumer-loans/credit-line&quot; target=&quot;blank&quot;&gt;  
                    Learn more  
                    &lt;svg width=&quot;14&quot; height=&quot;12&quot; viewBox=&quot;0 0 14 12&quot; fill=&quot;none&quot; xmlns=&quot;http://www.w3.org/2000/svg&quot;&gt;

                    &lt;/svg&gt;  
                &lt;/a&gt;  
            &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;/div&gt;  
        &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary&quot;&gt;  
            &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__content&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;div class=&quot;card-primary__header&quot;&gt;  
                    &lt;h3&gt;MyPay&lt;/h3&gt;  
                    &lt;img class=&quot;first-section&quot; src=&quot;https://ameriabank.am/Portals/0/Images/icons/credit-limit-icon.png&quot; alt=&quot;credit-limit-icon&quot;&gt;  
                &lt;/div&gt;  
                &lt;div&gt;  
                    &lt;p&gt;  
                        MyPay limit is the amount pre-approved by the Bank that you can use to make payments at the Bank’s partner shops.  
  
  
                    &lt;/p&gt;

            &lt;/div&gt;
